# SP97_WK_1 — Consolidated changes

Reading/state/placement differences and resolutions of uncertainties explicitly marked in C are counted. A row can differ from both witnesses. Resolving C’s uncertainty is recorded even when C’s first candidate survives. A later audit adjustment of our own uncertainty, with the same primary reading, adds no new witness-change row.

## Consequential corrections

It is distinct from the enclosure above. The latter word begins e-x-a and continues in the expected visible cursive groups, rather than est identique.
